Your focus, in plain English

The most repeated relationship theme is specialist insight. In work and practical choices, the birth month puts particular emphasis on opportunities and wider money. Your close-relationship layer adds specialist insight; these are different parts of the life pattern.

Your personal strength in this reading is You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result.

Try first: Read the personal style together with the work and hidden layers, rather than treating one symbol as the whole person.

Pause and clarify if: treating an attractive possibility as money already secured

Review after the attempt: Compare this traditional reading with the actual situation before making a consequential decision.

Personal Style and Career

The personal starting point is quality and attention to detail; the public work relationship is opportunities and wider money. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

Does the role allow this contribution with enough authority and resources?

Personal Needs and Close Relationships

The day-stem need is kind, clear communication and respect for personal preferences; the day-branch theme is being heard before someone offers a solution. These are separate layers of the same chart.

Does the actual relationship make room for both needs?

01 · What Shapes Your Life and Destiny?

The most repeated relationship theme is specialist insight. In work and practical choices, the birth month puts particular emphasis on opportunities and wider money. Your close-relationship layer adds specialist insight; these are different parts of the life pattern.

Your starting point is Yin Metal, the element and polarity of your birth-day stem. The traditional image is a carefully polished piece of jewellery. This reading points to quality and attention to detail; it becomes most useful when you have kind, clear communication and respect for personal preferences.

You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result. You may keep fixing small details, even when the work is already good enough. That can make it harder to finish.

Your Yin Metal day stem has same-element roots in hour branch 丑. In traditional terms, that gives the day element a foothold within the branches; the roots are not all equally strong.

The birth-month branch is 卯, associated with Wood. This is the seasonal setting around your day element, not an equal-strength vote in an element total. The report keeps that seasonal fact separate from the visible counts.

specialist insight is most repeated in the checked positions (3 of 7 each). This is a count of symbols, not a weighted strength or fortune score.

Your birth-month stem describes commercial openings, contacts and resources beyond a fixed routine. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

The principal stem within the birth month repeats opportunities and wider money. The public role and the routine underneath it therefore emphasise the same traditional relationship.

Only within the hidden stems, the chart also contains steady output, independence, pressure and decisive action, responsibility and recognised roles. These are less visible in this comparison and may be expressed through the responsibilities or setting rather than the first impression.

Some parts of the chart offer support while others place demands on it. This reading keeps the opportunity and its burden separate rather than treating the whole chart as strong or weak.

Key practical point: Read the personal style together with the work and hidden layers, rather than treating one symbol as the whole person.

Main caution: treating an attractive possibility as money already secured

02 · What Helps You Thrive — and What Holds You Back?

Your personal strength in this reading is You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result.

The corresponding blind spot is You may keep fixing small details, even when the work is already good enough. That can make it harder to finish.

At work, opportunities and wider money gives this strength a setting: Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task. The pressure is treating an attractive possibility as money already secured.

Around other people, learning and dependable support adds training, guidance and a foundation that makes work possible. A useful contribution can become a burden when it turns into the role you are always expected to carry.

In private life, the need is being heard before someone offers a solution; the friction is solving a problem before asking what help is wanted. The same person can handle a public role well and still need a different kind of support at home.

Key practical point: Distinguish a strength you choose to use from a role other people keep assigning to you.

Main caution: waiting for more reassurance or preparation when a usable foundation already exists

03 · How Are You Meant to Make Money?

Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

The wider support assessment is unresolved. This report does not turn the presence of money symbols into a verdict that the money can be managed easily.

The checked positions are month stem 乙 (opportunities and wider money); month branch 卯 (principal stem) (opportunities and wider money); year branch 寅 (principal stem) (regular money and commitments). The hidden check finds 乙 in the day branch 未; 甲 in the month branch 卯; 乙 in the month branch 卯; 甲 in the year branch 寅. Hidden entries are supporting evidence, not additional strength points.

No visible output relationship accompanies this check. The report therefore does not claim a ready-made chain from personal output to wealth; qualifications, an organisation or a commercial arrangement may still provide real income.

No peer relationship appears in these checked positions. That removes this particular sharing-and-competition theme; it does not remove ordinary contractual or financial risks.

Key practical point: Separate regular income, variable opportunities and shared obligations using actual figures.

Main caution: No traditional money relationship guarantees earnings, property or investment returns.

04 · Where Could You Find Your Kind of Success?

The career direction centres on opportunities and wider money. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

The routine underneath it is opportunities and wider money. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

The year stem adds learning and dependable support in the wider-group layer. Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation.

The central career tension is treating an attractive possibility as money already secured. A suitable field still needs a role with enough authority, training and compensation for its responsibilities.

Key practical point: Assess the daily role and its conditions alongside your real qualifications.

Main caution: treating an attractive possibility as money already secured

05 · What Do You Need in Love?

Your day branch 未 is the traditional close-relationship position. Its principal relationship is specialist insight. Privacy and being understood without constant explanation can matter. Withdrawing to think can look like emotional distance to someone expecting an immediate answer.

The branch image points to being heard before someone offers a solution. Listen first, then offer help

The friction to recognise is solving a problem before asking what help is wanted. A possible everyday expression is one person offering the kind of care they value while the other is waiting for a different response.

Birth hour / day contains a clash. Traditionally, the clash points to a part of life that is less settled: competing schedules, a change in responsibility or a different personal preference are possible expressions.

Birth day / month contains a clash and a partial combination. Traditionally, the clash points to a part of life that is less settled: competing schedules, a change in responsibility or a different personal preference are possible expressions.

Birth day / year contains a 鬼門. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

This is your side of a relationship reading. It cannot establish another person’s feelings, promise marriage or determine the timing of a relationship without additional assumptions.

Key practical point: Judge the relationship by actual behaviour, mutual consent and how both people respond.

Main caution: solving a problem before asking what help is wanted

06 · What Helps You Feel More Balanced?

The visible element picture is Wood 3, Fire 0, Earth 4, Metal 1, Water 0 across 8 symbols. Earth is most frequent in this unweighted count.

The birth-month branch supplies a Wood seasonal setting. A season, a visible symbol and a hidden root are different kinds of information. Adding them into a single score would imply a weighting method this report does not calculate.

Fire and Water are absent from the visible count. Hidden stems can differ from the visible picture. A zero does not mean a missing ability, a medical problem or an element you need to buy or consume.

Variety can energise you while leaving too many open commitments competing for attention.

Key practical point: Use the element picture as a temperament image, without treating a count as a prescription.

Main caution: This is not a physical or mental health reading.

07 · When Might Your Life Change Direction?

The structural change signals need to be read by the area they touch. The month layer concerns work and routine; the day layer concerns personal choices and close relationships. A signal in one area does not automatically describe every part of life.

In the checked 2026–2030 window, 2026, 2027 carry the relationships relevant to responsibility, work and money-related decisions. These are dates with a matching topic, not promises that the event will occur.

The birth-month pillar meets a clash at the 2029 annual references. A changed role, schedule or working arrangement is a possible expression; the comparison cannot establish a resignation, dismissal or business move.

From the calculated start 2021-12-22 until the next start 2031-12-22: learning and dependable support, with pressure and decisive action in the branch. Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation. This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

The annual timeline below shows each calculated comparison separately. A recorded combination can draw an arrangement together, while a clash can expose competing demands; neither supplies a guaranteed turning-point event.

Key practical point: Separate what may change in work, money or a close relationship before treating it as an all-or-nothing life decision.

Main caution: A change signal does not prove a future loss, separation or illness.

This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

Work and public role: Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation.

Money and commitments: The connection is indirect: qualifications, support or preparation can make paid work possible. Preparation also has a cost, especially when another course delays using an existing skill. Financial pressure can come through targets, penalties or a large commitment. This symbol describes demands rather than an amount you will earn or lose.

Love and private life: Intensity and decisiveness can attract, but pressure can spill into controlling the pace of a conversation. Firmness needs room for the other person to disagree.

Chart terms in plain English

比肩 / 劫財: These compare the day stem with stems of the same element. In this report they introduce questions about independence, shared decisions and working alongside others.

食神: These describe the element produced by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of making, expressing ideas and improving what you create.

正財 / 偏財: These describe the element controlled by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of managing everyday resources or exploring opportunities; they do not state how wealthy someone is.

正官 / 偏官: These describe the element that controls the day stem. Here they introduce questions about expectations, responsibility and responding under pressure.

正印 / 偏印: These describe the element that produces the day stem. Here they introduce themes of learning, receiving support and finding your own way to understand something.

Ten-year cycle / 大運: A traditional sequence calculated from the birth month, year-stem polarity and selected male/female cycle setting. The recorded start date is a calculation reference, not a promised event date.

Combination / 合: A traditional relationship between chart symbols. It is a prompt to explore how needs can work together, not proof of an easy relationship.

Clash / 沖: A directly opposing pair of chart symbols. Use it to ask about competing needs; it does not establish that conflict has happened.

Friction pattern / 刑: A traditional pattern name. In the naming convention used here, 子卯 is 無禮, 寅巳申 is 無恩 and 丑戌未 is 恃勢. The latter two are three-branch families; a matched pair must not be described as a complete three-branch group.

Disruption / 破 / 害: Names for symbol-pair relationships in the checked tables. They are not diagnoses, damage scores or predictions of harm.

Wonjin / 怨嗔 / 鬼門: Traditional pair-marker names. Here they are source labels for reflection on friction or sensitivity, a way to discuss sensitivity and misunderstandings without deciding what either person has experienced.

How this reading is prepared

Structural interpretation policy: FATE-qualitative-r1. Provisional evidence-led review edition; not a certified expert reading.

Traditional cycle setting: Male. The calculated year stem 戊 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run forward. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Recorded local clock: 1998-03-25 03:00 in America/New_York. Calculation clock: 1998-03-25 02:57 (-3 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 己丑 is selected in the 01:30-03:29 calculation-clock window. Across 03:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 己丑 / 庚寅. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Near a conventional hour boundary: the calculation clock is 3 minutes from 03:00. This engine instead changes hours at 03:30 (己丑 / 庚寅). A different hour convention or an uncertain birth time can change the result.

Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

This report contains no diagnosis, treatment or health-event prediction.

This is a traditional Saju interpretation of calculated symbols, not a factual assessment of your personality or a validated prediction. The situations describe possible expressions, not events known to have happened.

Emphasis means repetition in named positions. Visible relationships, principal branch stems and all hidden stems are listed separately; they are not added into a fortune score. A hidden principal stem also appears in the principal-branch comparison and is not an extra independent vote.

Visible counts are not a full assessment of day-master strength. This report does not claim to diagnose that from counts. The separate FATE-qualitative-r1 rule requires agreement between the month, roots and exposed relationships. Mixed, unknown-hour and exceptional cases are deferred. It is a provisional interpretation policy, not a universal school or a validated outcome model.

A month-led structure candidate and a support-versus-demand balancing candidate group answer different questions. Neither is presented as a confirmed useful element. Climate adjustment, special structures, full structure success and element transformation remain unresolved.

A money relationship is a traditional topic, not a forecast of income or return. A clash is not a forecast of loss, illness or separation; a combination does not guarantee cooperation or marriage.

Isolated two-branch comparisons do not claim a complete three-branch punishment group. The report retains directly checked clashes and other pair relationships.

Annual and monthly comparison points use 12:00 Asia/Seoul as one shared calculation reference. This is separate from the birthplace time zone used for the birth chart; it does not move an overseas birth to Seoul.

Annual references use 15 July and monthly references use the 15th, each at 12:00 Asia/Seoul. They are comparison points, not the start or the full span of a Gregorian year or month. Solar-term boundaries can change the pillar within a calendar month.

Your focus, in plain English

Person A experiences Person B through learning and dependable support; Person B experiences Person A through expression and challenge. These are directional comparisons, so the relationship can place different demands on each person.

01 · How Do You Fit Together?

Person A experiences Person B through learning and dependable support; Person B experiences Person A through expression and challenge. These are directional comparisons, so the relationship can place different demands on each person.

Birth-day comparison has no recorded pair pattern. That removes this particular structural signal; it does not establish an easy relationship or a quiet period.

Person A starts from Yin Metal; Person B starts from Yang Earth. Person A’s image is a carefully polished piece of jewellery; Person B’s is solid ground that others can stand on.

Person A’s day branch 未 points to being heard before someone offers a solution. Person B’s 申 points to listening as well as practical help. The private-life themes differ, so the same gesture of care may not feel equally useful to both people.

The structural picture is more informative than a compatibility percentage: attraction, practical fit and the ability to handle disagreement are separate questions. No calculated score has been added.

02 · Why Are You Drawn to Each Other?

From Person A’s reference, Person B represents learning and dependable support: training, guidance and a foundation that makes work possible. Care is read through reassurance, continuity and feeling understood. Support becomes uneven if one person is always the teacher or caretaker.

From Person B’s reference, Person A represents expression and challenge: original output, direct speech and questioning an existing rule. You are read as needing an honest exchange rather than polite silence. The difficulty is that a useful observation can arrive in words that sound like a verdict on the other person.

The labels differ because the reference person changes. A connection can feel supportive from one side and demanding from the other without either comparison being the whole story.

Person A’s caution is waiting for more reassurance or preparation when a usable foundation already exists; Person B’s is correcting the person before clarifying the problem. In daily life, these can meet in a disagreement about who decides, who supports or who has already contributed enough.

Keep close:

Give each person a separate account of the same interaction before deciding what it means.

Watch out for:

Directional relationships do not tell us who loves more or who should have authority.

Key practical point: Give each person a separate account of the same interaction before deciding what it means.

From the chart: A → B: 辛 comparing 戊 = 正印. B → A: 戊 comparing 辛 = 傷官.

03 · Why Do You See Things Differently?

Person A’s day branch 未 points to being heard before someone offers a solution. Person B’s 申 points to listening as well as practical help. The private-life themes differ, so the same gesture of care may not feel equally useful to both people.

For Person A: Privacy and being understood without constant explanation can matter. Withdrawing to think can look like emotional distance to someone expecting an immediate answer.

For Person B: Care tends to be shown through ordinary acts: making time, feeding someone or making life comfortable. A quieter pace can be mistaken for a lack of ambition or interest.

The practical tension for Person A is solving a problem before asking what help is wanted; for Person B it is offering an answer before hearing how the problem feels. Those are interpretations of the day-branch layer, not a record of what either person has done.

Keep close:

Name the form of care each person wants rather than using the chart to speak for them.

Watch out for:

The chart cannot establish another person’s intentions or consent.

Key practical point: Name the form of care each person wants rather than using the chart to speak for them.

04 · Can Your Everyday Lives Fit Together?

Person A’s routine has opportunities and wider money above opportunities and wider money; Person B’s has pressure and decisive action above steady output.

Person A: Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

Person B: Craft, teaching, food, design and repeatable technical work are examples. The common thread is turning a skill into a dependable result rather than relying on status alone.

Birth-month comparison contains a 怨嗔 and a 鬼門. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

Compare private choices separately from the work of running a day together. The day and month checks describe different layers, even when their pattern labels happen to match.

Keep close:

Make ownership of recurring tasks and the less visible follow-up explicit.

Watch out for:

A month-pillar pattern does not establish who actually does more work at home.

Key practical point: Make ownership of recurring tasks and the less visible follow-up explicit.

05 · Can You Agree About Money?

Person A: Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

Person B: Money relationships appear inside the branches but not in the checked visible or principal positions. Money is a quieter layer in this comparison, rather than the chart’s most obvious outward theme.

From A’s side, the connection brings learning and dependable support. The connection is indirect: qualifications, support or preparation can make paid work possible. Preparation also has a cost, especially when another course delays using an existing skill.

From B’s side, the connection brings expression and challenge. Income themes come through distinctive work, visibility or a persuasive explanation. Attention is not the same as paid demand, and outspoken disagreement can complicate a contract.

Money compatibility cannot be reduced to the presence of a money symbol. An actual shared cost needs agreement about contribution, ownership and what happens if one person’s circumstances change.

Keep close:

Distinguish a gift, a loan and a shared expense before committing funds.

Watch out for:

Do not use one person’s chart to decide their financial worth or entitlement to shared money.

Key practical point: Distinguish a gift, a loan and a shared expense before committing funds.

08 · What Does This Connection Ask of You?

The directional themes are learning and dependable support for Person A and expression and challenge for Person B. The private-life comparison and the daily routine need to be assessed separately.

The key directional cautions are waiting for more reassurance or preparation when a usable foundation already exists for Person A and correcting the person before clarifying the problem for Person B. The useful question is whether the actual relationship makes room for both people’s needs, rather than whether a symbol certifies the match.

Keep close:

Agree on one concrete difference in time, money or responsibility that both people can describe.

Watch out for:

Use actual behaviour and safety to judge a relationship; no symbol overrides them.

Key practical point: Agree on one concrete difference in time, money or responsibility that both people can describe.

Your Everyday Compass

Questions you may be asking

How Do You Fit Together?: what is the direct answer?

Person A experiences Person B through learning and dependable support; Person B experiences Person A through expression and challenge. These are directional comparisons, so the relationship can place different demands on each person.

Why this appears: Person A: day pillar 辛未. Stem 辛: 本元 (starting point); branch 未: 偏印 (Independent learning).

Why Are You Drawn to Each Other?: what is the direct answer?

From Person A’s reference, Person B represents learning and dependable support: training, guidance and a foundation that makes work possible. Care is read through reassurance, continuity and feeling understood. Support becomes uneven if one person is always the teacher or caretaker.

Why this appears: A → B: 辛 comparing 戊 = 正印.

Why Do You See Things Differently?: what is the direct answer?

Person A’s day branch 未 points to being heard before someone offers a solution. Person B’s 申 points to listening as well as practical help. The private-life themes differ, so the same gesture of care may not feel equally useful to both people.

Bring closer

How Do You Fit Together?: Compare the described difference with how both people actually make a shared decision.

Why Are You Drawn to Each Other?: Give each person a separate account of the same interaction before deciding what it means.

Why Do You See Things Differently?: Name the form of care each person wants rather than using the chart to speak for them.

Give less space to

How Do You Fit Together?: A combination does not prove attraction, and a clash does not make someone a bad partner.

Why Are You Drawn to Each Other?: Directional relationships do not tell us who loves more or who should have authority.

Why Do You See Things Differently?: The chart cannot establish another person’s intentions or consent.

Person B · Earth

Numbers: 5 / 10

These colours and numbers are traditional symbols for your birth-day element. Use them as personal reminders. They do not change the odds of an event or promise a result.

A reminder to keep: Use the reading to name a traditional pattern. Actual choices, consent and circumstances determine what happens.

Before a consequential decision

1. Compare the described difference with how both people actually make a shared decision.

2. Give each person a separate account of the same interaction before deciding what it means.

3. Name the form of care each person wants rather than using the chart to speak for them.

Cultural references

Five-colour tradition · Korea.net: https://www.korea.net/NewsFocus/HonoraryReporters/view?articleId=218521

He Tu number pairs · historical text: https://zh.wikisource.org/wiki/河圖原

Chart terms in plain English

比肩 / 劫財: These compare the day stem with stems of the same element. In this report they introduce questions about independence, shared decisions and working alongside others.

食神 / 傷官: These describe the element produced by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of making, expressing ideas and improving what you create.

正財 / 偏財: These describe the element controlled by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of managing everyday resources or exploring opportunities; they do not state how wealthy someone is.

正官 / 偏官: These describe the element that controls the day stem. Here they introduce questions about expectations, responsibility and responding under pressure.

正印 / 偏印: These describe the element that produces the day stem. Here they introduce themes of learning, receiving support and finding your own way to understand something.

Combination / 合: A traditional relationship between chart symbols. It is a prompt to explore how needs can work together, not proof of an easy relationship.

Clash / 沖: A directly opposing pair of chart symbols. Use it to ask about competing needs; it does not establish that conflict has happened.

Friction pattern / 刑: A traditional pattern name. In the naming convention used here, 子卯 is 無禮, 寅巳申 is 無恩 and 丑戌未 is 恃勢. The latter two are three-branch families; a matched pair must not be described as a complete three-branch group.

Disruption / 破 / 害: Names for symbol-pair relationships in the checked tables. They are not diagnoses, damage scores or predictions of harm.

Wonjin / 怨嗔 / 鬼門: Traditional pair-marker names. Here they are source labels for reflection on friction or sensitivity, a way to discuss sensitivity and misunderstandings without deciding what either person has experienced.

How this reading is prepared

Structural interpretation policy: FATE-qualitative-r1. Provisional evidence-led review edition; not a certified expert reading.

Traditional cycle setting: Male. The calculated year stem 戊 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run forward. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Recorded local clock: 1998-03-25 03:00 in America/New_York. Calculation clock: 1998-03-25 02:57 (-3 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 己丑 is selected in the 01:30-03:29 calculation-clock window. Across 03:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 己丑 / 庚寅. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Near a conventional hour boundary: the calculation clock is 3 minutes from 03:00. This engine instead changes hours at 03:30 (己丑 / 庚寅). A different hour convention or an uncertain birth time can change the result.

Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

Person B: Traditional cycle setting: Female. The calculated year stem 庚 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run reverse. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Person B: Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Person B: Recorded local clock: 2000-08-18 14:30 in Europe/London. Calculation clock: 2000-08-18 13:26 (-64 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Person B: Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 戊午 is selected in the 11:30-13:29 calculation-clock window. Across 13:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 戊午 / 己未. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Person B: Near an hour boundary: the calculation clock is 4 minutes from this engine's 13:30 boundary. The pillars on its two sides are 戊午 / 己未. Check the recorded birth time before relying on hour-sensitive details.

Person B: Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

This report contains no diagnosis, treatment or health-event prediction.

This is a traditional Saju interpretation of calculated symbols, not a factual assessment of your personality or a validated prediction. The situations describe possible expressions, not events known to have happened.

Emphasis means repetition in named positions. Visible relationships, principal branch stems and all hidden stems are listed separately; they are not added into a fortune score. A hidden principal stem also appears in the principal-branch comparison and is not an extra independent vote.

Visible counts are not a full assessment of day-master strength. This report does not claim to diagnose that from counts. The separate FATE-qualitative-r1 rule requires agreement between the month, roots and exposed relationships. Mixed, unknown-hour and exceptional cases are deferred. It is a provisional interpretation policy, not a universal school or a validated outcome model.

A month-led structure candidate and a support-versus-demand balancing candidate group answer different questions. Neither is presented as a confirmed useful element. Climate adjustment, special structures, full structure success and element transformation remain unresolved.

A money relationship is a traditional topic, not a forecast of income or return. A clash is not a forecast of loss, illness or separation; a combination does not guarantee cooperation or marriage.

Isolated two-branch comparisons do not claim a complete three-branch punishment group. The report retains directly checked clashes and other pair relationships.

Annual and monthly comparison points use 12:00 Asia/Seoul as one shared calculation reference. This is separate from the birthplace time zone used for the birth chart; it does not move an overseas birth to Seoul.

Annual references use 15 July and monthly references use the 15th, each at 12:00 Asia/Seoul. They are comparison points, not the start or the full span of a Gregorian year or month. Solar-term boundaries can change the pillar within a calendar month.

01 · What Is the Main Story of Your Year?

2027 adds pressure and decisive action in the annual stem and specialist insight in the principal branch stem. Demanding targets, competition and responding to a difficult situation become the traditional headline; this is not an overall good-or-bad luck rating.

Crisis handling, high-responsibility operations, competitive sales and demanding technical delivery are examples. Clear authority and adequate resources matter because urgency alone is not a workable job design. The annual stem differs from your birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money. The year therefore adds a different demand to the natal pattern.

2027-07-15: No favourable-or-unfavourable verdict is assigned: the natal balance or an active longer cycle is unresolved. A checked clash, punishment, harm or break adds structural friction; a supportive balance reading therefore still has a caution.

Reference / birth hour contains a clash. Traditionally, the clash points to a part of life that is less settled: competing schedules, a change in responsibility or a different personal preference are possible expressions.

Reference / birth month contains a partial combination. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

These are topics and structural signals, not lists of profitable or unlucky months.

02 · What Could Shape Your Money This Year?

For 2027, Financial pressure can come through targets, penalties or a large commitment. This symbol describes demands rather than an amount you will earn or lose.

This is a knowledge symbol rather than a direct money symbol. Specialist insight needs a clear use and a paying audience before it becomes an income source.

Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

The monthly references with direct money relationships are February 2027, March 2027, April 2027, May 2027. The presence of a money relationship is not a measure of profit, and its absence is not a prediction of financial difficulty.

03 · Where Could Work Take You This Year?

The work theme for 2027 is pressure and decisive action. Crisis handling, high-responsibility operations, competitive sales and demanding technical delivery are examples. Clear authority and adequate resources matter because urgency alone is not a workable job design.

The underlying annual layer is specialist insight. Specialist research, analysis, strategy and work requiring an uncommon perspective are examples. Protected thinking time matters more than a role filled with immediate responses.

Your natal work stem remains opportunities and wider money. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

The main work caution in the annual layer is escalating urgency before checking the authority and resources available. A role change, promotion or dismissal is not established by this relationship; the traditional reading identifies the kind of demand to examine.

Keep close:

Check the actual remit, resources and recognition attached to a new responsibility.

Watch out for:

escalating urgency before checking the authority and resources available

Key practical point: Check the actual remit, resources and recognition attached to a new responsibility.

04 · What Could This Year Bring to Your Love Life?

The annual stem brings pressure and decisive action. Intensity and decisiveness can attract, but pressure can spill into controlling the pace of a conversation. Firmness needs room for the other person to disagree.

The principal annual branch stem brings specialist insight. Privacy and being understood without constant explanation can matter. Withdrawing to think can look like emotional distance to someone expecting an immediate answer.

Reference / birth day has no recorded pair pattern. That removes this particular structural signal; it does not establish an easy relationship or a quiet period.

Your natal day branch 未 still points to being heard before someone offers a solution. The annual pattern adds a temporary comparison; it does not replace the natal relationship layer or tell us how another person feels.

Keep close:

Separate changes to shared time or responsibilities from assumptions about someone’s feelings.

Watch out for:

Key practical point: Separate changes to shared time or responsibilities from assumptions about someone’s feelings.

No month meets the stated criteria for a more supportive reference. The natal balance may be unresolved, the timing layers may disagree, or a separate friction signal may remain. No good month is invented to fill the list.

From the calculated start 2021-12-22 until the next start 2031-12-22: learning and dependable support, with pressure and decisive action in the branch. Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation. The money-topic months remain a separate list: a month can contain commercial activity without being easier to manage. The twelve monthly chapters explain work, money and private life together.

What Is the Main Story of Your Year?: what is the direct answer?

Where Could Work Take You This Year?: what is the direct answer?

The work theme for 2027 is pressure and decisive action. Crisis handling, high-responsibility operations, competitive sales and demanding technical delivery are examples. Clear authority and adequate resources matter because urgency alone is not a workable job design.

Chart terms in plain English

比肩 / 劫財: These compare the day stem with stems of the same element. In this report they introduce questions about independence, shared decisions and working alongside others.

食神 / 傷官: These describe the element produced by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of making, expressing ideas and improving what you create.

正財 / 偏財: These describe the element controlled by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of managing everyday resources or exploring opportunities; they do not state how wealthy someone is.

正官 / 偏官: These describe the element that controls the day stem. Here they introduce questions about expectations, responsibility and responding under pressure.

正印 / 偏印: These describe the element that produces the day stem. Here they introduce themes of learning, receiving support and finding your own way to understand something.

Combination / 合: A traditional relationship between chart symbols. It is a prompt to explore how needs can work together, not proof of an easy relationship.

Clash / 沖: A directly opposing pair of chart symbols. Use it to ask about competing needs; it does not establish that conflict has happened.

Friction pattern / 刑: A traditional pattern name. In the naming convention used here, 子卯 is 無禮, 寅巳申 is 無恩 and 丑戌未 is 恃勢. The latter two are three-branch families; a matched pair must not be described as a complete three-branch group.

Disruption / 破 / 害: Names for symbol-pair relationships in the checked tables. They are not diagnoses, damage scores or predictions of harm.

Wonjin / 怨嗔 / 鬼門: Traditional pair-marker names. Here they are source labels for reflection on friction or sensitivity, a way to discuss sensitivity and misunderstandings without deciding what either person has experienced.

How this reading is prepared

Structural interpretation policy: FATE-qualitative-r1. Provisional evidence-led review edition; not a certified expert reading.

Traditional cycle setting: Male. The calculated year stem 戊 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run forward. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Recorded local clock: 1998-03-25 03:00 in America/New_York. Calculation clock: 1998-03-25 02:57 (-3 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 己丑 is selected in the 01:30-03:29 calculation-clock window. Across 03:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 己丑 / 庚寅. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Near a conventional hour boundary: the calculation clock is 3 minutes from 03:00. This engine instead changes hours at 03:30 (己丑 / 庚寅). A different hour convention or an uncertain birth time can change the result.

Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

This report contains no diagnosis, treatment or health-event prediction.

This is a traditional Saju interpretation of calculated symbols, not a factual assessment of your personality or a validated prediction. The situations describe possible expressions, not events known to have happened.

Emphasis means repetition in named positions. Visible relationships, principal branch stems and all hidden stems are listed separately; they are not added into a fortune score. A hidden principal stem also appears in the principal-branch comparison and is not an extra independent vote.

Visible counts are not a full assessment of day-master strength. This report does not claim to diagnose that from counts. The separate FATE-qualitative-r1 rule requires agreement between the month, roots and exposed relationships. Mixed, unknown-hour and exceptional cases are deferred. It is a provisional interpretation policy, not a universal school or a validated outcome model.

A month-led structure candidate and a support-versus-demand balancing candidate group answer different questions. Neither is presented as a confirmed useful element. Climate adjustment, special structures, full structure success and element transformation remain unresolved.

A money relationship is a traditional topic, not a forecast of income or return. A clash is not a forecast of loss, illness or separation; a combination does not guarantee cooperation or marriage.

Isolated two-branch comparisons do not claim a complete three-branch punishment group. The report retains directly checked clashes and other pair relationships.

Annual and monthly comparison points use 12:00 Asia/Seoul as one shared calculation reference. This is separate from the birthplace time zone used for the birth chart; it does not move an overseas birth to Seoul.

Annual references use 15 July and monthly references use the 15th, each at 12:00 Asia/Seoul. They are comparison points, not the start or the full span of a Gregorian year or month. Solar-term boundaries can change the pillar within a calendar month.

01 · Where Could Your Working Strengths Take You?

Your career pattern centres on opportunities and wider money, repeated in both birth-month layers. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

Your starting point is Yin Metal, the element and polarity of your birth-day stem. The traditional image is a carefully polished piece of jewellery. This reading points to quality and attention to detail; it becomes most useful when you have kind, clear communication and respect for personal preferences.

You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result. You may keep fixing small details, even when the work is already good enough. That can make it harder to finish.

Your Yin Metal day stem has same-element roots in hour branch 丑. In traditional terms, that gives the day element a foothold within the branches; the roots are not all equally strong.

The birth-month branch is 卯, associated with Wood. This is the seasonal setting around your day element, not an equal-strength vote in an element total. The report keeps that seasonal fact separate from the visible counts.

Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

The chart’s recurring work pressure is treating an attractive possibility as money already secured. Changing the employer while keeping the same working conditions can leave that pressure unchanged.

Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation. A clash can accompany movement or disagreement, but it does not tell us whether leaving is better than staying. The annual reading is compared with the active longer cycle in the timing chapter.

Chart terms in plain English

比肩 / 劫財: These compare the day stem with stems of the same element. In this report they introduce questions about independence, shared decisions and working alongside others.

食神: These describe the element produced by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of making, expressing ideas and improving what you create.

正財 / 偏財: These describe the element controlled by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of managing everyday resources or exploring opportunities; they do not state how wealthy someone is.

正官 / 偏官: These describe the element that controls the day stem. Here they introduce questions about expectations, responsibility and responding under pressure.

正印 / 偏印: These describe the element that produces the day stem. Here they introduce themes of learning, receiving support and finding your own way to understand something.

Combination / 合: A traditional relationship between chart symbols. It is a prompt to explore how needs can work together, not proof of an easy relationship.

Clash / 沖: A directly opposing pair of chart symbols. Use it to ask about competing needs; it does not establish that conflict has happened.

Friction pattern / 刑: A traditional pattern name. In the naming convention used here, 子卯 is 無禮, 寅巳申 is 無恩 and 丑戌未 is 恃勢. The latter two are three-branch families; a matched pair must not be described as a complete three-branch group.

Disruption / 破 / 害: Names for symbol-pair relationships in the checked tables. They are not diagnoses, damage scores or predictions of harm.

Wonjin / 怨嗔 / 鬼門: Traditional pair-marker names. Here they are source labels for reflection on friction or sensitivity, a way to discuss sensitivity and misunderstandings without deciding what either person has experienced.

How this reading is prepared

Structural interpretation policy: FATE-qualitative-r1. Provisional evidence-led review edition; not a certified expert reading.

Traditional cycle setting: Male. The calculated year stem 戊 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run forward. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Recorded local clock: 1998-03-25 03:00 in America/New_York. Calculation clock: 1998-03-25 02:57 (-3 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 己丑 is selected in the 01:30-03:29 calculation-clock window. Across 03:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 己丑 / 庚寅. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Near a conventional hour boundary: the calculation clock is 3 minutes from 03:00. This engine instead changes hours at 03:30 (己丑 / 庚寅). A different hour convention or an uncertain birth time can change the result.

Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

This report contains no diagnosis, treatment or health-event prediction.

This is a traditional Saju interpretation of calculated symbols, not a factual assessment of your personality or a validated prediction. The situations describe possible expressions, not events known to have happened.

Emphasis means repetition in named positions. Visible relationships, principal branch stems and all hidden stems are listed separately; they are not added into a fortune score. A hidden principal stem also appears in the principal-branch comparison and is not an extra independent vote.

Visible counts are not a full assessment of day-master strength. This report does not claim to diagnose that from counts. The separate FATE-qualitative-r1 rule requires agreement between the month, roots and exposed relationships. Mixed, unknown-hour and exceptional cases are deferred. It is a provisional interpretation policy, not a universal school or a validated outcome model.

A month-led structure candidate and a support-versus-demand balancing candidate group answer different questions. Neither is presented as a confirmed useful element. Climate adjustment, special structures, full structure success and element transformation remain unresolved.

A money relationship is a traditional topic, not a forecast of income or return. A clash is not a forecast of loss, illness or separation; a combination does not guarantee cooperation or marriage.

Isolated two-branch comparisons do not claim a complete three-branch punishment group. The report retains directly checked clashes and other pair relationships.

Annual and monthly comparison points use 12:00 Asia/Seoul as one shared calculation reference. This is separate from the birthplace time zone used for the birth chart; it does not move an overseas birth to Seoul.

Annual references use 15 July and monthly references use the 15th, each at 12:00 Asia/Seoul. They are comparison points, not the start or the full span of a Gregorian year or month. Solar-term boundaries can change the pillar within a calendar month.

01 · Were You Born to Build a Business?

The business pattern is opportunities and wider money, repeated in both birth-month layers. Customer acquisition, trading and several channels are relevant expressions. Expansion becomes harder to manage when new orders arrive before delivery and cash collection are settled. This is a founder style, not a yes-or-no verdict on commercial success.

As a founder, your Yin Metal day-stem image points to quality and attention to detail. A possible expression is how you set the direction and make decisions about your own offer; the image is not proof of entrepreneurial ability.

The personal working condition is kind, clear communication and respect for personal preferences. In a business, that needs room in delivery and customer communication as well as in the original idea.

The founder’s public role comes through opportunities and wider money; the delivery routine underneath it comes through opportunities and wider money. Customer acquisition, trading and several channels are relevant expressions. Expansion becomes harder to manage when new orders arrive before delivery and cash collection are settled.

Some parts of the chart offer support while others place demands on it. This reading keeps the opportunity and its burden separate rather than treating the whole chart as strong or weak.

Keep close:

Assess the offer and delivery model separately from the wish to work for yourself.

Watch out for:

treating an attractive possibility as money already secured

Key practical point: Assess the offer and delivery model separately from the wish to work for yourself.

03 · Could a Business Partner Help or Complicate Things?

Your wider-group relationship is learning and dependable support. Expertise-based services, education and a documented method fit this theme. Delivery can become slow if more research keeps being added before the offer reaches a customer.

The birth chart repeats specialist insight most often in the checked positions. Repetition can make that way of handling a team more persistent; it does not decide who should own the business.

The available birth pillars contain 3 pair comparisons with a recorded relationship. These are separate checks, not a total measure of conflict or luck.

Birth hour / day contains a clash. Traditionally, the clash points to a part of life that is less settled: competing schedules, a change in responsibility or a different personal preference are possible expressions.

Birth day / month contains a clash and a partial combination. Traditionally, the clash points to a part of life that is less settled: competing schedules, a change in responsibility or a different personal preference are possible expressions.

Birth day / year contains a 鬼門. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

No complete three-branch or directional combination was found in the available natal branches. A two-branch partial pattern is not promoted into a complete group.

The practical distinction is between attraction or attachment and friction or movement. A chart can contain both. These describe traditional tensions within the chart, not a record of family events or a prediction that a relationship will end.

Keep close:

Define decision rights, money ownership and the work each person is responsible for.

Watch out for:

This one-person chart does not calculate a co-founder match, an equity split or a company chart.

Key practical point: Define decision rights, money ownership and the work each person is responsible for.

04 · How Could Your Business Earn and Keep Money?

Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

The wider support assessment is unresolved. This report does not turn the presence of money symbols into a verdict that the money can be managed easily.

The checked positions are month stem 乙 (opportunities and wider money); month branch 卯 (principal stem) (opportunities and wider money); year branch 寅 (principal stem) (regular money and commitments). The hidden check finds 乙 in the day branch 未; 甲 in the month branch 卯; 乙 in the month branch 卯; 甲 in the year branch 寅. Hidden entries are supporting evidence, not additional strength points.

No visible output relationship accompanies this check. The report therefore does not claim a ready-made chain from personal output to wealth; qualifications, an organisation or a commercial arrangement may still provide real income.

No peer relationship appears in these checked positions. That removes this particular sharing-and-competition theme; it does not remove ordinary contractual or financial risks.

06 · What Do Your Ten-Year Business Cycles Reveal?

From the calculated start 2021-12-22 until the next start 2031-12-22: learning and dependable support, with pressure and decisive action in the branch. Expertise-based services, education and a documented method fit this theme. Delivery can become slow if more research keeps being added before the offer reaches a customer. This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

From the calculated start 2031-12-22 until the next start 2041-12-22: specialist insight, with specialist insight in the branch. A niche method or specialist advisory offer fits this pattern. The challenge is translating complexity into a result a customer understands and can use. This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

From the calculated start 2041-12-22 until the next start 2051-12-22: competition and shared resources, with competition and shared resources in the branch. Sales partnerships, referrals and networks are relevant. Revenue sharing, ownership and access to the customer list need particular clarity. This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

2026 — responsibility and recognised roles, with pressure and decisive action underneath. A business built on trust, compliance or consistent service fits this theme. Delegation needs an accountable owner; informal promises can undermine a professional offer. 2026-07-15: No favourable-or-unfavourable verdict is assigned: the natal balance or an active longer cycle is unresolved. A checked clash, punishment, harm or break adds structural friction; a supportive balance reading therefore still has a caution.

A recorded pair pattern touches the birth hour and day and month and year pillars; the detailed evidence records which pattern.

2027 — pressure and decisive action, with specialist insight underneath. Turnaround work, demanding delivery and a competitive market fit this pattern. Your Everyday Compass

Questions you may be asking

Were You Born to Build a Business?: what is the direct answer?

The business pattern is opportunities and wider money, repeated in both birth-month layers. Customer acquisition, trading and several channels are relevant expressions. Expansion becomes harder to manage when new orders arrive before delivery and cash collection are settled. This is a founder style, not a yes-or-no verdict on commercial success.

Why this appears: day pillar 辛未. Stem 辛: 本元 (starting point); branch 未: 偏印 (Independent learning).

What Does Your Chart Say About Starting Out?: what is the direct answer?

The starting-business pattern is opportunities and wider money. Customer acquisition, trading and several channels are relevant expressions. Expansion becomes harder to manage when new orders arrive before delivery and cash collection are settled.

Why this appears: month pillar 乙卯. Stem 乙: 偏財 (Opportunity and exchange); branch 卯: 偏財 (Opportunity and exchange).

Could a Business Partner Help or Complicate Things?: what is the direct answer?

Your wider-group relationship is learning and dependable support. Expertise-based services, education and a documented method fit this theme. Delivery can become slow if more research keeps being added before the offer reaches a customer.

Why this appears: year pillar 戊寅. Stem 戊: 正印 (Support and knowledge); branch 寅: 正財 (Steady resources).

Chart terms in plain English

比肩 / 劫財: These compare the day stem with stems of the same element. In this report they introduce questions about independence, shared decisions and working alongside others.

食神: These describe the element produced by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of making, expressing ideas and improving what you create.

正財 / 偏財: These describe the element controlled by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of managing everyday resources or exploring opportunities; they do not state how wealthy someone is.

正官 / 偏官: These describe the element that controls the day stem. Here they introduce questions about expectations, responsibility and responding under pressure.

正印 / 偏印: These describe the element that produces the day stem. Here they introduce themes of learning, receiving support and finding your own way to understand something.

Combination / 合: A traditional relationship between chart symbols. It is a prompt to explore how needs can work together, not proof of an easy relationship.

Clash / 沖: A directly opposing pair of chart symbols. Use it to ask about competing needs; it does not establish that conflict has happened.

Friction pattern / 刑: A traditional pattern name. In the naming convention used here, 子卯 is 無禮, 寅巳申 is 無恩 and 丑戌未 is 恃勢. The latter two are three-branch families; a matched pair must not be described as a complete three-branch group.

Disruption / 破 / 害: Names for symbol-pair relationships in the checked tables. They are not diagnoses, damage scores or predictions of harm.

Wonjin / 怨嗔 / 鬼門: Traditional pair-marker names. Here they are source labels for reflection on friction or sensitivity, a way to discuss sensitivity and misunderstandings without deciding what either person has experienced.

How this reading is prepared

Structural interpretation policy: FATE-qualitative-r1. Provisional evidence-led review edition; not a certified expert reading.

Traditional cycle setting: Male. The calculated year stem 戊 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run forward. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Recorded local clock: 1998-03-25 03:00 in America/New_York. Calculation clock: 1998-03-25 02:57 (-3 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 己丑 is selected in the 01:30-03:29 calculation-clock window. Across 03:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 己丑 / 庚寅. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Near a conventional hour boundary: the calculation clock is 3 minutes from 03:00. This engine instead changes hours at 03:30 (己丑 / 庚寅). A different hour convention or an uncertain birth time can change the result.

Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

This report contains no diagnosis, treatment or health-event prediction.

This is a traditional Saju interpretation of calculated symbols, not a factual assessment of your personality or a validated prediction. The situations describe possible expressions, not events known to have happened.

Emphasis means repetition in named positions. Visible relationships, principal branch stems and all hidden stems are listed separately; they are not added into a fortune score. A hidden principal stem also appears in the principal-branch comparison and is not an extra independent vote.

Visible counts are not a full assessment of day-master strength. This report does not claim to diagnose that from counts. The separate FATE-qualitative-r1 rule requires agreement between the month, roots and exposed relationships. Mixed, unknown-hour and exceptional cases are deferred. It is a provisional interpretation policy, not a universal school or a validated outcome model.

A month-led structure candidate and a support-versus-demand balancing candidate group answer different questions. Neither is presented as a confirmed useful element. Climate adjustment, special structures, full structure success and element transformation remain unresolved.

A money relationship is a traditional topic, not a forecast of income or return. A clash is not a forecast of loss, illness or separation; a combination does not guarantee cooperation or marriage.

Isolated two-branch comparisons do not claim a complete three-branch punishment group. The report retains directly checked clashes and other pair relationships.

Annual and monthly comparison points use 12:00 Asia/Seoul as one shared calculation reference. This is separate from the birthplace time zone used for the birth chart; it does not move an overseas birth to Seoul.

Annual references use 15 July and monthly references use the 15th, each at 12:00 Asia/Seoul. They are comparison points, not the start or the full span of a Gregorian year or month. Solar-term boundaries can change the pillar within a calendar month.

Your starting point is Yin Metal, the element and polarity of your birth-day stem. The traditional image is a carefully polished piece of jewellery. This reading points to quality and attention to detail; it becomes most useful when you have kind, clear communication and respect for personal preferences.

You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result. You may keep fixing small details, even when the work is already good enough. That can make it harder to finish.

Your Yin Metal day stem has same-element roots in hour branch 丑. In traditional terms, that gives the day element a foothold within the branches; the roots are not all equally strong.

The birth-month branch is 卯, associated with Wood. This is the seasonal setting around your day element, not an equal-strength vote in an element total. The report keeps that seasonal fact separate from the visible counts.

04 · What Do You Need From the People Around You?

The day branch 未 gives the close-relationship theme of being heard before someone offers a solution. Listen first, then offer help

The traditional friction is solving a problem before asking what help is wanted. In daily life, this can become a mismatch between the support offered and the space or reassurance wanted.

The available birth pillars contain 3 pair comparisons with a recorded relationship. These are separate checks, not a total measure of conflict or luck.

Birth hour / day contains a clash. Traditionally, the clash points to a part of life that is less settled: competing schedules, a change in responsibility or a different personal preference are possible expressions.

Birth day / year contains a 鬼門. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

No complete three-branch or directional combination was found in the available natal branches. A two-branch partial pattern is not promoted into a complete group.

The practical distinction is between attraction or attachment and friction or movement. A chart can contain both. These describe traditional tensions within the chart, not a record of family events or a prediction that a relationship will end.

Chart terms in plain English

比肩: These compare the day stem with stems of the same element. In this report they introduce questions about independence, shared decisions and working alongside others.

食神: These describe the element produced by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of making, expressing ideas and improving what you create.

正財 / 偏財: These describe the element controlled by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of managing everyday resources or exploring opportunities; they do not state how wealthy someone is.

正官 / 偏官: These describe the element that controls the day stem. Here they introduce questions about expectations, responsibility and responding under pressure.

正印 / 偏印: These describe the element that produces the day stem. Here they introduce themes of learning, receiving support and finding your own way to understand something.

Combination / 合: A traditional relationship between chart symbols. It is a prompt to explore how needs can work together, not proof of an easy relationship.

Clash / 沖: A directly opposing pair of chart symbols. Use it to ask about competing needs; it does not establish that conflict has happened.

Disruption / 破 / 害: Names for symbol-pair relationships in the checked tables. They are not diagnoses, damage scores or predictions of harm.

Wonjin / 怨嗔 / 鬼門: Traditional pair-marker names. Here they are source labels for reflection on friction or sensitivity, a way to discuss sensitivity and misunderstandings without deciding what either person has experienced.

How this reading is prepared

Structural interpretation policy: FATE-qualitative-r1. Provisional evidence-led review edition; not a certified expert reading.

Traditional cycle setting: Male. The calculated year stem 戊 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run forward. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Recorded local clock: 1998-03-25 03:00 in America/New_York. Calculation clock: 1998-03-25 02:57 (-3 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 己丑 is selected in the 01:30-03:29 calculation-clock window. Across 03:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 己丑 / 庚寅. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Near a conventional hour boundary: the calculation clock is 3 minutes from 03:00. This engine instead changes hours at 03:30 (己丑 / 庚寅). A different hour convention or an uncertain birth time can change the result.

Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

This report contains no diagnosis, treatment or health-event prediction.

This is a traditional Saju interpretation of calculated symbols, not a factual assessment of your personality or a validated prediction. The situations describe possible expressions, not events known to have happened.

Emphasis means repetition in named positions. Visible relationships, principal branch stems and all hidden stems are listed separately; they are not added into a fortune score. A hidden principal stem also appears in the principal-branch comparison and is not an extra independent vote.

Visible counts are not a full assessment of day-master strength. This report does not claim to diagnose that from counts. The separate FATE-qualitative-r1 rule requires agreement between the month, roots and exposed relationships. Mixed, unknown-hour and exceptional cases are deferred. It is a provisional interpretation policy, not a universal school or a validated outcome model.

A month-led structure candidate and a support-versus-demand balancing candidate group answer different questions. Neither is presented as a confirmed useful element. Climate adjustment, special structures, full structure success and element transformation remain unresolved.

A money relationship is a traditional topic, not a forecast of income or return. A clash is not a forecast of loss, illness or separation; a combination does not guarantee cooperation or marriage.

Isolated two-branch comparisons do not claim a complete three-branch punishment group. The report retains directly checked clashes and other pair relationships.

Annual and monthly comparison points use 12:00 Asia/Seoul as one shared calculation reference. This is separate from the birthplace time zone used for the birth chart; it does not move an overseas birth to Seoul.

Annual references use 15 July and monthly references use the 15th, each at 12:00 Asia/Seoul. They are comparison points, not the start or the full span of a Gregorian year or month. Solar-term boundaries can change the pillar within a calendar month.



# Your Destiny Collection

## How Do Your Life, Love and Year Connect?

How Do Your Life, Love and Year Connect?

This reading connects Person A’s life pattern, the relationship between both people and Person A’s selected year. It separates what lasts from what changes, then brings the three readings together around the choices they share.

The lasting pattern: Yin Metal; birth-month opportunities and wider money, with opportunities and wider money underneath. Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

The relationship: Person A experiences Person B through learning and dependable support; Person B experiences Person A through expression and challenge. These are directional comparisons, so the relationship can place different demands on each person. Birth-day comparison has no recorded pair pattern. That removes this particular structural signal; it does not establish an easy relationship or a quiet period.

The annual layer: 2027 adds pressure and decisive action in the annual stem and specialist insight in the principal branch stem. Demanding targets, competition and responding to a difficult situation become the traditional headline; this is not an overall good-or-bad luck rating. Read its twelve monthly comparisons as a temporary layer around Person A’s natal pattern.

These layers answer different questions: the life reading describes the natal pattern and longer cycles; compatibility describes how two charts meet; the annual reading describes a calculated reference year. Repeated themes reinforce a topic, not the probability of a promised event.

01 · What Shapes Your Life and Destiny?

The most repeated relationship theme is specialist insight. In work and practical choices, the birth month puts particular emphasis on opportunities and wider money. Your close-relationship layer adds specialist insight; these are different parts of the life pattern.

Your starting point is Yin Metal, the element and polarity of your birth-day stem. The traditional image is a carefully polished piece of jewellery. This reading points to quality and attention to detail; it becomes most useful when you have kind, clear communication and respect for personal preferences.

You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result. You may keep fixing small details, even when the work is already good enough. That can make it harder to finish.

Your Yin Metal day stem has same-element roots in hour branch 丑. In traditional terms, that gives the day element a foothold within the branches; the roots are not all equally strong.

The birth-month branch is 卯, associated with Wood. This is the seasonal setting around your day element, not an equal-strength vote in an element total. The report keeps that seasonal fact separate from the visible counts.

specialist insight is most repeated in the checked positions (3 of 7 each). This is a count of symbols, not a weighted strength or fortune score.

Your birth-month stem describes commercial openings, contacts and resources beyond a fixed routine. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

The principal stem within the birth month repeats opportunities and wider money. The public role and the routine underneath it therefore emphasise the same traditional relationship.

Only within the hidden stems, the chart also contains steady output, independence, pressure and decisive action, responsibility and recognised roles. These are less visible in this comparison and may be expressed through the responsibilities or setting rather than the first impression.

Some parts of the chart offer support while others place demands on it. This reading keeps the opportunity and its burden separate rather than treating the whole chart as strong or weak.

Key practical point: Read the personal style together with the work and hidden layers, rather than treating one symbol as the whole person.

Main caution: treating an attractive possibility as money already secured

02 · What Helps You Thrive — and What Holds You Back?

Your personal strength in this reading is You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result.

The corresponding blind spot is You may keep fixing small details, even when the work is already good enough. That can make it harder to finish.

At work, opportunities and wider money gives this strength a setting: Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task. The pressure is treating an attractive possibility as money already secured.

Around other people, learning and dependable support adds training, guidance and a foundation that makes work possible. A useful contribution can become a burden when it turns into the role you are always expected to carry.

In private life, the need is being heard before someone offers a solution; the friction is solving a problem before asking what help is wanted. The same person can handle a public role well and still need a different kind of support at home.

Key practical point: Distinguish a strength you choose to use from a role other people keep assigning to you.

Main caution: waiting for more reassurance or preparation when a usable foundation already exists
