05 · What Do You Need in Love?

Your day branch 未 is the traditional close-relationship position. Its principal relationship is specialist insight. Privacy and being understood without constant explanation can matter. Withdrawing to think can look like emotional distance to someone expecting an immediate answer.

The branch image points to being heard before someone offers a solution. Listen first, then offer help

The friction to recognise is solving a problem before asking what help is wanted. A possible everyday expression is one person offering the kind of care they value while the other is waiting for a different response.

This is your side of a relationship reading. It cannot establish another person’s feelings, promise marriage or determine the timing of a relationship without additional assumptions.

Key practical point: Judge the relationship by actual behaviour, mutual consent and how both people respond.

The structural change signals need to be read by the area they touch. The month layer concerns work and routine; the day layer concerns personal choices and close relationships. A signal in one area does not automatically describe every part of life.

Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation. This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

The annual timeline below shows each calculated comparison separately. A recorded combination can draw an arrangement together, while a clash can expose competing demands; neither supplies a guaranteed turning-point event.

Key practical point: Separate what may change in work, money or a close relationship before treating it as an all-or-nothing life decision.

Main caution: A change signal does not prove a future loss, separation or illness.

From the calculated start 2021-12-22 until the next start 2031-12-22: learning and dependable support, with pressure and decisive action in the branch. Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation. This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

Work and public role: Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation.

Money and commitments: The connection is indirect: qualifications, support or preparation can make paid work possible. Preparation also has a cost, especially when another course delays using an existing skill. Financial pressure can come through targets, penalties or a large commitment. This symbol describes demands rather than an amount you will earn or lose.

Love and private life: Intensity and decisiveness can attract, but pressure can spill into controlling the pace of a conversation. Firmness needs room for the other person to disagree.

Your Everyday Compass

Questions you may be asking

What Shapes Your Life and Destiny?: what is the direct answer?

The most repeated relationship theme is specialist insight. In work and practical choices, the birth month puts particular emphasis on opportunities and wider money. Your close-relationship layer adds specialist insight; these are different parts of the life pattern.

Why this appears: day pillar 辛未. Stem 辛: 本元 (starting point); branch 未: 偏印 (Independent learning).

What Helps You Thrive — and What Holds You Back?: what is the direct answer?

Your personal strength in this reading is You may have a sharp eye for small details. You notice words, colours or finishing touches that improve the result.

How Are You Meant to Make Money?: what is the direct answer?

Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

Why this appears: hour stem 己: 偏印 (specialist insight).

Cultural references

Chart terms in plain English

比肩 / 劫財: These compare the day stem with stems of the same element. In this report they introduce questions about independence, shared decisions and working alongside others.

食神: These describe the element produced by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of making, expressing ideas and improving what you create.

正財 / 偏財: These describe the element controlled by the day stem. Here they introduce themes of managing everyday resources or exploring opportunities; they do not state how wealthy someone is.

正官 / 偏官: These describe the element that controls the day stem. Here they introduce questions about expectations, responsibility and responding under pressure.

正印 / 偏印: These describe the element that produces the day stem. Here they introduce themes of learning, receiving support and finding your own way to understand something.

Ten-year cycle / 大運: A traditional sequence calculated from the birth month, year-stem polarity and selected male/female cycle setting. The recorded start date is a calculation reference, not a promised event date.

Combination / 合: A traditional relationship between chart symbols. It is a prompt to explore how needs can work together, not proof of an easy relationship.

Clash / 沖: A directly opposing pair of chart symbols. Use it to ask about competing needs; it does not establish that conflict has happened.

Friction pattern / 刑: A traditional pattern name. In the naming convention used here, 子卯 is 無禮, 寅巳申 is 無恩 and 丑戌未 is 恃勢. The latter two are three-branch families; a matched pair must not be described as a complete three-branch group.

Disruption / 破 / 害: Names for symbol-pair relationships in the checked tables. They are not diagnoses, damage scores or predictions of harm.

Wonjin / 怨嗔 / 鬼門: Traditional pair-marker names. Here they are source labels for reflection on friction or sensitivity, a way to discuss sensitivity and misunderstandings without deciding what either person has experienced.

How this reading is prepared

Structural interpretation policy: FATE-qualitative-r1. Provisional evidence-led review edition; not a certified expert reading.

Traditional cycle setting: Male. The calculated year stem 戊 is yang, so the ten-year cycles run forward. This setting determines cycle direction; it is not a personality judgement.

Local time is resolved with its IANA time zone, including historical daylight saving, then adjusted for longitude and the approximate equation of time.

Recorded local clock: 1998-03-25 03:00 in America/New_York. Calculation clock: 1998-03-25 02:57 (-3 minutes from the entered local clock). The calculation clock is a convention-based value, not a second recorded birth time.

Hour convention: this engine uses half-hour-offset two-hour windows, including 01:30 and 03:30. Here 己丑 is selected in the 01:30-03:29 calculation-clock window. Across 03:30, the adjacent hour pillars are 己丑 / 庚寅. Other calculators may use the conventional odd-hour boundaries instead.

Near a conventional hour boundary: the calculation clock is 3 minutes from 03:00. This engine instead changes hours at 03:30 (己丑 / 庚寅). A different hour convention or an uncertain birth time can change the result.

Late-night day boundary: unified Zi-hour convention (the day changes at 23:30 on the calculation clock).

This report contains no diagnosis, treatment or health-event prediction.

This is a traditional Saju interpretation of calculated symbols, not a factual assessment of your personality or a validated prediction. The situations describe possible expressions, not events known to have happened.

Emphasis means repetition in named positions. Visible relationships, principal branch stems and all hidden stems are listed separately; they are not added into a fortune score. A hidden principal stem also appears in the principal-branch comparison and is not an extra independent vote.

Visible counts are not a full assessment of day-master strength. This report does not claim to diagnose that from counts. The separate FATE-qualitative-r1 rule requires agreement between the month, roots and exposed relationships. Mixed, unknown-hour and exceptional cases are deferred. It is a provisional interpretation policy, not a universal school or a validated outcome model.

A month-led structure candidate and a support-versus-demand balancing candidate group answer different questions. Neither is presented as a confirmed useful element. Climate adjustment, special structures, full structure success and element transformation remain unresolved.

A money relationship is a traditional topic, not a forecast of income or return. A clash is not a forecast of loss, illness or separation; a combination does not guarantee cooperation or marriage.

Isolated two-branch comparisons do not claim a complete three-branch punishment group. The report retains directly checked clashes and other pair relationships.

Annual and monthly comparison points use 12:00 Asia/Seoul as one shared calculation reference. This is separate from the birthplace time zone used for the birth chart; it does not move an overseas birth to Seoul.

Annual references use 15 July and monthly references use the 15th, each at 12:00 Asia/Seoul. They are comparison points, not the start or the full span of a Gregorian year or month. Solar-term boundaries can change the pillar within a calendar month.

Your focus, in plain English

Person A experiences Person B through learning and dependable support; Person B experiences Person A through expression and challenge. These are directional comparisons, so the relationship can place different demands on each person.

From Person A’s reference, Person B represents learning and dependable support: training, guidance and a foundation that makes work possible. Care is read through reassurance, continuity and feeling understood. Support becomes uneven if one person is always the teacher or caretaker.

Try first: Compare the described difference with how both people actually make a shared decision.

Pause and clarify if: A combination does not prove attraction, and a clash does not make someone a bad partner.

Review after the attempt: Compare this traditional reading with the actual situation before making a consequential decision.

01 · How Do You Fit Together?

Person A experiences Person B through learning and dependable support; Person B experiences Person A through expression and challenge. These are directional comparisons, so the relationship can place different demands on each person.

Birth-day comparison has no recorded pair pattern. That removes this particular structural signal; it does not establish an easy relationship or a quiet period.

Person A starts from Yin Metal; Person B starts from Yang Earth. Person A’s image is a carefully polished piece of jewellery; Person B’s is solid ground that others can stand on.

Person A’s day branch 未 points to being heard before someone offers a solution. Person B’s 申 points to listening as well as practical help. The private-life themes differ, so the same gesture of care may not feel equally useful to both people.

The structural picture is more informative than a compatibility percentage: attraction, practical fit and the ability to handle disagreement are separate questions. No calculated score has been added.

Person A: Some parts of the chart offer support while others place demands on it. This reading keeps the opportunity and its burden separate rather than treating the whole chart as strong or weak.

Person B: Some parts of the chart offer support while others place demands on it. This reading keeps the opportunity and its burden separate rather than treating the whole chart as strong or weak.

Keep close:

Compare the described difference with how both people actually make a shared decision.

Watch out for:

A combination does not prove attraction, and a clash does not make someone a bad partner.

Key practical point: Compare the described difference with how both people actually make a shared decision.

02 · Why Are You Drawn to Each Other?

From Person A’s reference, Person B represents learning and dependable support: training, guidance and a foundation that makes work possible. Care is read through reassurance, continuity and feeling understood. Support becomes uneven if one person is always the teacher or caretaker.

From Person B’s reference, Person A represents expression and challenge: original output, direct speech and questioning an existing rule. You are read as needing an honest exchange rather than polite silence. The difficulty is that a useful observation can arrive in words that sound like a verdict on the other person.

The labels differ because the reference person changes. A connection can feel supportive from one side and demanding from the other without either comparison being the whole story.

Person A’s caution is waiting for more reassurance or preparation when a usable foundation already exists; Person B’s is correcting the person before clarifying the problem. In daily life, these can meet in a disagreement about who decides, who supports or who has already contributed enough.

Keep close:

Give each person a separate account of the same interaction before deciding what it means.

Watch out for:

Directional relationships do not tell us who loves more or who should have authority.

Key practical point: Give each person a separate account of the same interaction before deciding what it means.

From the chart: A → B: 辛 comparing 戊 = 正印. B → A: 戊 comparing 辛 = 傷官.

03 · Why Do You See Things Differently?

Person A’s day branch 未 points to being heard before someone offers a solution. Person B’s 申 points to listening as well as practical help. The private-life themes differ, so the same gesture of care may not feel equally useful to both people.

For Person A: Privacy and being understood without constant explanation can matter. Withdrawing to think can look like emotional distance to someone expecting an immediate answer.

For Person B: Care tends to be shown through ordinary acts: making time, feeding someone or making life comfortable. A quieter pace can be mistaken for a lack of ambition or interest.

The practical tension for Person A is solving a problem before asking what help is wanted; for Person B it is offering an answer before hearing how the problem feels. Those are interpretations of the day-branch layer, not a record of what either person has done.

Keep close:

Name the form of care each person wants rather than using the chart to speak for them.

Watch out for:

The chart cannot establish another person’s intentions or consent.

Key practical point: Name the form of care each person wants rather than using the chart to speak for them.

04 · Can Your Everyday Lives Fit Together?

Person A’s routine has opportunities and wider money above opportunities and wider money; Person B’s has pressure and decisive action above steady output.

Person A: Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

Person B: Craft, teaching, food, design and repeatable technical work are examples. The common thread is turning a skill into a dependable result rather than relying on status alone.

Birth-month comparison contains a 怨嗔 and a 鬼門. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

Compare private choices separately from the work of running a day together. The day and month checks describe different layers, even when their pattern labels happen to match.

Keep close:

Make ownership of recurring tasks and the less visible follow-up explicit.

Watch out for:

A month-pillar pattern does not establish who actually does more work at home.

Key practical point: Make ownership of recurring tasks and the less visible follow-up explicit.

05 · Can You Agree About Money?

Person A: Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

Person B: Money relationships appear inside the branches but not in the checked visible or principal positions. Money is a quieter layer in this comparison, rather than the chart’s most obvious outward theme.

From A’s side, the connection brings learning and dependable support. The connection is indirect: qualifications, support or preparation can make paid work possible. Preparation also has a cost, especially when another course delays using an existing skill.

From B’s side, the connection brings expression and challenge. Income themes come through distinctive work, visibility or a persuasive explanation. Attention is not the same as paid demand, and outspoken disagreement can complicate a contract.

Money compatibility cannot be reduced to the presence of a money symbol. An actual shared cost needs agreement about contribution, ownership and what happens if one person’s circumstances change.

Keep close:

Distinguish a gift, a loan and a shared expense before committing funds.

Watch out for:

Do not use one person’s chart to decide their financial worth or entitlement to shared money.

Key practical point: Distinguish a gift, a loan and a shared expense before committing funds.

08 · What Does This Connection Ask of You?

The directional themes are learning and dependable support for Person A and expression and challenge for Person B. The private-life comparison and the daily routine need to be assessed separately.

The key directional cautions are waiting for more reassurance or preparation when a usable foundation already exists for Person A and correcting the person before clarifying the problem for Person B. The useful question is whether the actual relationship makes room for both people’s needs, rather than whether a symbol certifies the match.

Keep close:

Agree on one concrete difference in time, money or responsibility that both people can describe.

Watch out for:

Use actual behaviour and safety to judge a relationship; no symbol overrides them.

Key practical point: Agree on one concrete difference in time, money or responsibility that both people can describe.

01 · What Is the Main Story of Your Year?

2027 adds pressure and decisive action in the annual stem and specialist insight in the principal branch stem. Demanding targets, competition and responding to a difficult situation become the traditional headline; this is not an overall good-or-bad luck rating.

Crisis handling, high-responsibility operations, competitive sales and demanding technical delivery are examples. Clear authority and adequate resources matter because urgency alone is not a workable job design. The annual stem differs from your birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money. The year therefore adds a different demand to the natal pattern.

2027-07-15: No favourable-or-unfavourable verdict is assigned: the natal balance or an active longer cycle is unresolved. A checked clash, punishment, harm or break adds structural friction; a supportive balance reading therefore still has a caution.

Reference / birth hour contains a clash. Traditionally, the clash points to a part of life that is less settled: competing schedules, a change in responsibility or a different personal preference are possible expressions.

Reference / birth month contains a partial combination. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

Reference / birth year contains a 鬼門. Traditionally, these patterns draw attention to repeated friction, crossed expectations or an arrangement that needs revising.

Direct money relationships appear in the sampled monthly layers of February 2027, March 2027, April 2027, May 2027. These are topics and structural signals, not lists of profitable or unlucky months.

02 · What Could Shape Your Money This Year?

For 2027, Financial pressure can come through targets, penalties or a large commitment. This symbol describes demands rather than an amount you will earn or lose.

This is a knowledge symbol rather than a direct money symbol. Specialist insight needs a clear use and a paying audience before it becomes an income source.

Both regular-money and opportunity-money relationships are visible. The traditional picture has two routes: maintaining dependable commitments and dealing with customers or openings beyond the fixed routine.

The monthly references with direct money relationships are February 2027, March 2027, April 2027, May 2027. The presence of a money relationship is not a measure of profit, and its absence is not a prediction of financial difficulty.

Use confirmed income, costs and the actual terms of an offer for financial decisions.

Watch out for:

Do not use a symbolic month label to choose an investment, borrowing or an unaffordable commitment.

Key practical point: Use confirmed income, costs and the actual terms of an offer for financial decisions.

03 · Where Could Work Take You This Year?

The work theme for 2027 is pressure and decisive action. Crisis handling, high-responsibility operations, competitive sales and demanding technical delivery are examples. Clear authority and adequate resources matter because urgency alone is not a workable job design.

The underlying annual layer is specialist insight. Specialist research, analysis, strategy and work requiring an uncommon perspective are examples. Protected thinking time matters more than a role filled with immediate responses.

Your natal work stem remains opportunities and wider money. Sales, account development, sourcing, brokerage and project-based commercial work are examples. Relationships and judging an opening matter more here than repeating a narrow task.

The main work caution in the annual layer is escalating urgency before checking the authority and resources available. A role change, promotion or dismissal is not established by this relationship; the traditional reading identifies the kind of demand to examine.

Keep close:

Check the actual remit, resources and recognition attached to a new responsibility.

Watch out for:

escalating urgency before checking the authority and resources available

Key practical point: Check the actual remit, resources and recognition attached to a new responsibility.

04 · What Could This Year Bring to Your Love Life?

The annual stem brings pressure and decisive action. Intensity and decisiveness can attract, but pressure can spill into controlling the pace of a conversation. Firmness needs room for the other person to disagree.

The principal annual branch stem brings specialist insight. Privacy and being understood without constant explanation can matter. Withdrawing to think can look like emotional distance to someone expecting an immediate answer.

Reference / birth day has no recorded pair pattern. That removes this particular structural signal; it does not establish an easy relationship or a quiet period.

Your natal day branch 未 still points to being heard before someone offers a solution. The annual pattern adds a temporary comparison; it does not replace the natal relationship layer or tell us how another person feels.

Keep close:

Separate changes to shared time or responsibilities from assumptions about someone’s feelings.

Watch out for:

A day-pillar clash does not establish separation, and a combination does not establish marriage.

Key practical point: Separate changes to shared time or responsibilities from assumptions about someone’s feelings.

No month meets the stated criteria for a more supportive reference. The natal balance may be unresolved, the timing layers may disagree, or a separate friction signal may remain. No good month is invented to fill the list.

From the calculated start 2021-12-22 until the next start 2031-12-22: learning and dependable support, with pressure and decisive action in the branch. Education, research, documentation, professional training and advisory support are examples. Depth and recognised knowledge matter more than constant improvisation. This differs from the birth-month stem’s opportunities and wider money, so the longer period adds a different demand to the natal work pattern.

The money-topic months remain a separate list: a month can contain commercial activity without being easier to manage. The twelve monthly chapters explain work, money and private life together.
